## Protocol

- Close owner `tiered_tp_pct` with one tier `{"tp_tiers": [{"close_fraction": 1.0, "profit_pct": 0.03}]}`, comparison mode `strict`, stop owner fixed entry ATR x1.0 (simple ATR), capital $1000, leverage 1, `ohlc_walk` intrabar resolution; signals on bar N fill at the bar N+1 open.
- Arms: `smi_long` (`stochastic_momentum_index`, long), `smi_both` (`stochastic_momentum_index`, both), `stoch_rsi` (`stoch_rsi`, long), `mean_reversion_pro` (`mean_reversion_pro`, both). Matched comparisons: `smi_long` vs `stoch_rsi` (long only) and `smi_both` vs `mean_reversion_pro` (both directions).
- Selection on venue `train` only: every dataset scored, complete candle and funding coverage on every leg, no liquidated leg, at least min_train_independent_positions independent position clusters; order max mean net Sharpe; max mean drawdown-adjusted return; earliest grid order. The proxy evidence uses the venue selections; nothing is reselected from held-out or proxy results.
- Held-out gates: at least 30 independent position clusters per arm and at least 10 positions in each direction for the bidirectional arms. positions of all datasets of one arm and window are sorted by entry time and every overlapping holding interval (entry at or before the latest exit so far) merges into one cluster.
- Stability: grid combinations one index step away in exactly one parameter from the selected candidate parameters; frozen before scoring; at least two thirds of the evaluable one-step neighbours of each candidate arm have positive held-out net PnL at base cost; a neighbour is evaluable when every dataset scored with complete coverage; no reselection from held-out results.
- Pass rule: complete coverage and sample gates, no liquidated leg, both candidate arms net PnL > 0 at base and stress cost, smi_long base mean Sharpe and base mean drawdown-adjusted return both strictly above stoch_rsi, smi_both both strictly above mean_reversion_pro, and the stability rule met for both candidate arms.
- `stoch_rsi` keeps its registered long-only entries: its -1 is a long exit and is never treated as a short entry, so its comparison uses the matched long-only candidate arm.

## Finite replay versus a continuously seeded diagnostic

The diagnostic seeds every recursion once at the first complete range window of the whole manifest frame and carries it forward. ## Close validation

- Modes `strict`, eligibility `eligible`, parity status `unverified`, incomplete parity no.
- `unverified` is the strict, eligible capability status (no approximation and no refusal); it is not a ledger-verified claim that these exits equal live exits.

## Decision parity

`backtest/candidates/stochastic_momentum_index_1663/parity_results.json` sha256 `1ace5c8ab681741d` from `run_parity.py`, clean yes.

- composer_and_batched_paper: 16 compute_parity_frame runs (composer and explicit-paper solo/batch slots, 200-row window and full history, both directions with the frozen close owner, plus an entry-only domain endpoint): 7776 bars compared, 0 mismatches, 38 long and 30 short opens, clean True
- every_prefix: defaults, selected and domain-endpoint parameters on every prefix of the last 700 bars of each venue dataset: 42885 full-series vs prefix, 200-row and exact-span comparisons, 0 mismatches, 75 entry endpoints
- grid_sampled: all 729 grid combinations at ends [450, 600, 700] of the ETH 4h tail: 6561 comparisons, 0 mismatches
- Admission and timed closure are proved by the subprocess scenarios in `shared_scripts/test_stochastic_momentum_index_checks.py`; a parity run alone does not prove forming-row isolation.
